Approaches:

- Main local_search(p0, time_limit, seed, eta, K_frac, tau_frac):
   p = normalize(p0)
   current = score_fft(p)
   best = p,best_score
   eta=base (e.g. 0.5)
   K maybe start 512, grow? If current peak has many comparable coefficients, need larger K. Use Kfrac maybe 0.1 of L or dynamic based on concentrated top: if top K covers >? We can compute gap; if top 1 not much above next, increase K.
   tau_frac start 0.2 (smooth). Anneal to 0.02 over iterations/time.
   Also maybe randomize p occasionally (restart if stagnation).
   Loop while time:
      c = conv_self_fft(p)
      current = 2n*max(c)
      if current < best: update
      if time low break
      topK etc. d.
      if maxd==0: break
      # scale d by perhaps maxd? use d_scaled = d/maxd.
      # Optionally center? Multiplicative normalization effectively centers; but if d has large mean, all reduce. Fine.
      # Use adaptive eta: line search over [eta, eta*2, eta/2...]? Start with current eta; try step, half, quarter; if fail maybe double? Since nonconvex, try both.
      accepted=False
      for factor in [1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 2.0, 4.0?]:
         step=eta*factor
         p_try = p*np.exp(-step*d_scaled); floor, renormalize.
         score_try = score_fft(p_try)
         if finite and score_try < current*(1-1e-12): accept; eta *= (factor if factor>1 else 0.8?) maybe keep step
      if not accepted: eta*=0.5; if eta<1e-4 break
      p=p_try
   return best.

Let's implement manual Adam with line search perhaps. Need ensure not too slow.

Manual Adam in log domain:
   z = np.log(p) (p positive; add eps)
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
   learning rate base maybe 0.01? But z scale arbitrary. We can normalize z mean0. Update:
      gz = p*(d - dot(p,d))  # gradient of smooth max wrt z (unnormalized?) For p=softmax(z), ∂F/∂z_i = p_i d_i - ∑_j p_j d_j p_j = p_i(d_i - dbar_p). Yes.
      m=β1 m+(1-β1)gz; v=β2 v+(1-β2)gz^2
      mhat=m/(1-β1^t); vhat=v/(1-β2^t)
      z -= lr * mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
      z -= z.mean() # gauge
      p=softmax(z); clip.
   We need line search? Adam step fixed may diverge if lr too high. We can test candidate score; if not improve, reduce lr and maybe redo? Could do backtracking: start lr, if score worse, reduce lr and update. But Adam state update? Could be messy.
   Use normalized gradient: gz_norm = gz / (norm(gz)/n) maybe. lr ~0.1? Let's estimate gz: d~1/n, dbar~1/n, difference ~1/n; p~1/n -> gz~1/n^2 ~2.6e-8. Without scaling, lr must huge. Better scale gradient to unit norm: g = gz; norm = mean(g^2); if norm>0 g=g/ sqrt(norm). Then update z with lr ~0.01-0.1. After step, p changes O(lr). Use step.
   Objective score ~2n max c. Smooth F = logsumexp maybe scale; gradient sign. We'll just use score.
   Use backtracking line search: z_try = z - lr*g_norm. Compute score. If not improve, lr*=0.5 up to 5 times. If accept, lr*=1.05. Reset m,v? No.
   This is projected gradient in log domain. Since objective nonconvex, okay.

   But computing gz requires d. We can use direct active or dense. Direct active d approximate gradient of active weighted sum, not smooth full; sign okay. Use top K weights. For Adam, gradient from top K may be noisy; dense smooth maybe better. But direct active can focus. We can alternate: Use dense weights every `dense_every` iterations; direct top K otherwise.

   Dense gradient with weights w (sum1) gives d. Then gz = p*(d - dot(p,d)). Normalize. This is smooth if w=softmax(c/tau). Good.

   For nonconvex local minima, add random restarts. Use small random perturbations to best if stagnation.

Potential advantage: We can minimize smooth max with τ decreasing. The optimum of smooth approaches max. We can schedule tau based on improvement: start tau_frac=0.5 (peak*0.5), reduce to 0.01 as time goes. If tau too large, gradient uniform; maybe no improvement. Start moderate 0.1.

Let's define local_search_log(p0, max_time, base_tau_frac, dense_every, seed):
   n,len
   p=normalize; z=log(p+eps); z-=mean
   m/v zeros; lr=0.05; t=0
   current=score_fft(p); best=p,score
   tau_frac=base (e.g.0.15)
   no_improve_time=0
   while time:
      c = conv_self(p)
      current = 2n*max(c)
      if current<best: update no_improve=0
      # anneal tau: tau_frac = max(0.01, 0.15 * exp(-elapsed/300)) maybe.
      if dense_every condition: d = dense_gradient(c,tau); else d = direct_gradient(c,p,tau, K)
      dbar=np.dot(p,d)
      g=p*(d - dbar)
      gnrm=np.linalg.norm(g)/n? Use RMS = sqrt(mean(g*g))
      if RMS small break
      g = g / RMS
      # line search
      trial=False
      lr_use=lr
      for _ in range(6):
          z_try = z - lr_use*g
          # limit z_try range to prevent overflow: clip z_try to [minz-20,maxz+20]? Or clip to [-30,30]
          z_try = np.clip(z_try, -30,30)
          z_try -= z_try.mean()
          p_try = softmax(z_try)
          score_try = score_fft(p_try)
          if score_try < current - 1e-9*max(1,current):
              z = z_try; p=p_try; accepted=True; break
          lr_use *=0.5
      if accepted:
          # update Adam? We used gradient simple; just z moved. m/v maybe stale.
          lr = min(2.0, lr*1.1)
      else:
          lr=lr*0.5
          if lr<1e-5:
              # reset z to best with noise
              p = best copy; add log noise; break/restart
      # Maybe after accepting, occasionally normalize/clip p to avoid zeros: p = (1-1e-8)*p +1e-8/n
   return best

Pseudo code structure:
```
import numpy as np, time, math

def _now(): return time.monotonic()

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m<=1: return 1
    p=1
    while p<m: p*=2
    return p

def _normalize(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    p=np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return np.ones(p.size)/p.size
    return p/s

def _softmax(z):
   z=np.asarray(z); z=z-z.max(); z=np.clip(z,-50,50); e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); return e/s

def _conv_self_fft(p):
   n=p.size; L=2*n-1; nfft=_next_pow2(L)
   F=np.fft.rfft(p,nfft)
   c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,n=nfft)[:L]
   # eliminate tiny negatives
   return c
```
For dense gradient, if using nfft length 2n-1, R length L; conv length n+L-1=3n-2 > nfft. Can't reuse nfft. Need separate function.
```
def _grad_dense(c,p, q):
   n=p.size; L=2*n-1
   peak=np.max(c)
   base=np.clip(c,0,peak)/peak if peak>0 else np.ones(L)/L
   w=base**q
   ws=w.sum()
   if ws>0: w/=ws
   else: w=0; target=np.argmax(c); w[target]=1
   r=w[::-1]
   nfft=_next_pow2(n+L-1)
   F=np.fft.rfft(p,nfft)
   R=np.fft.rfft(r,nfft)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(F*R,n=nfft)
   d=2.0*conv[n-1:L][::-1]
   return d
```
But if we already computed c with nfft1 next_pow2(L) (16384), for gradient dense nfft2=32768. We can instead compute c using nfft2 once: `nfft=_next_pow2(3*n-2)` for all. Fp length nfft; c=irfft(Fp*Fp)[:L]. gradient uses same Fp, R. This doubles memory but okay (32768). For small n, choose common. Let's create `compute_c_and_grad(p, q, dense)`? To reduce recompute Fp.
```
def _c_and_gradient(p, q, dense):
   n=p.size; L=2*n-1; nfft=_next_pow2(3*n-2) # if dense else next_pow2(L)
```

Direct gradient:
```
def _grad_direct(c,p, q, K):
   n=p.size; L=2*n-1
   peak=float(np.max(c))
   if peak<=0: return np.zeros(n)
   if K>=c.size: idx=np.arange(c.size); K=c.size
   else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
   vals=c[idx]
   # weights base = vals/peak; if vals<=0 ->0
   base=np.clip(vals/peak, 0.0, 1.0)
   w=base**q
   ws=w.sum()
   if ws<=0 or not finite: w=np.zeros_like; w[np.argmax(vals)]=1
   else: w/=ws
   d=np.zeros(n)
   for k,wk in zip(idx,w):
      if wk<=0: continue
      k=int(k); f=2.0*float(wk)
      if k<n:
         d[:k+1] += f * p[k::-1]
      else:
         length=L-k
         start=k-n+1
         d[start:start+length] += f * p[n-1:start-1:-1]
   return d
```
Check slice for k=n: length=n-1, start=1, p[n-1:0:-1] length n-1. For k=L-1=2n-2: length=1, start= n-1, p[n-1:n-2:-1]=[p[n-1]]. Good.

Weights base**q: If vals near peak, w high. If q=10, second half negligible. Could be too top. Adjust q via tau_frac: q = max(1.0, 1/tau_frac) maybe. tau_frac=0.2 => q=5, smoother; tau_frac=0.05 =>20. Start q=5, anneal to 30. We can parameter `q`.

But if g has both pos/neg, some z decrease, some increase. Good.

Let's define helper `_eval_exact(p)`:

def _eval_exact(p):
    try:
        s=float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in p]))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    return s if np.isfinite(s) else np.inf
```

def _scan_initials(n, rng):
    inits=[]
    def add(p): p=_normalize(p); s=_score_fft(p); inits.append((s,p))
    # parent maybe
    add(np.ones(n))
    for lam in [...]: add(linear)
    for alpha in [...]: add(geometric)
    for freq in [1,2,3,4]: for phase in [0,0.7,1.5,2.5]: add(sin)
    for _ in range(8): add(random fourier)
    for _ in range(4): add(random exp)
    # optional smooth random bits
    inits.sort(key)
    return inits[:M]
```

Linear profile:
```
def _make_linear(n, l):
   if n==1: return ones
   x=np.linspace(-1,1,n)
   p=1.0 + l*x
   p=np.clip(p,1e-12,None)
   return p/p.sum()
```
Geometric:
```
def _make_geometric(n, alpha):
   t=np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1)
   p=np.exp(-alpha*t)  # decays
   return p/p.sum()
```

def _make_sinusoid(n, lam, freq, phase):
   t=np.arange(n)/n
   p=1.0 + lam*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*t + phase)
   # optionally add small quadratic to ensure nonneg
   p=np.clip(p,1e-12,None)
   return p/p.sum()
```

Now local_search details more precisely:
```
def _local_search(p0, deadline, rng, q0=5.0, q_min=20.0, dense_every=8, Kfrac=0.1, lr0=0.05, restart_iters=50):
    p=_normalize(p0)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_fft(p)
    z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
    lr=lr0
    q=q0
    no_improve=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=_conv_self_fft(p)  # fast nfft L
       peak_c=float(np.max(c))
       current=2.0*n*peak_c  # p sum1; but if sum not exactly 1 due drift, normalize p first? We normalize p periodically. use p.sum()
       if current < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=current; best=p.copy(); no_improve=0
       else: no_improve +=1
       if no_improve > 60:
           # perturb best
           z=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); z+=rng.normal(0,0.5,n); z-=z.mean(); p=_softmax(z); lr=lr0; continue
       # anneal q
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-start
       q=max(q_min, q0 - elapsed/30?) or q *=1.02? Better q = q0 * (1 + elapsed/120)
       # choose gradient
       if iteration % dense_every ==0:
           d=_grad_dense(c,p,q)
       else:
           K=max(32,min(n, int(Kfrac*n)))
           d=_grad_direct(c,p,q,K)
       dmax=np.max(d)
       if dmax <=1e-300 or not finite: break
       ds=d/dmax
       ddot=float(np.dot(p,ds))
       g=p*(ds-ddot)
       rms=np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g))
       if rms <=1e-300: break
       g/=rms
       accepted=False
       step=lr
       for _ in range(6):
           z_try=z - step*g
           z_try=np.clip(z_try, -40, 40)
           z_try -= z_try.mean()
           p_try=_softmax(z_try)
           sc=_score_fft_fast(p_try)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < current - 1e-9*max(1.0,current):
               z=z_try; p=p_try; accepted=True
               lr=min(2.0, lr*1.1)
               break
           step *=0.5
       if not accepted:
           lr*=0.5
           if lr<1e-4:
               z=np.log(np.maximum(best,1e-300)); z+=rng.normal(0,1.0,n); z-=z.mean(); p=_softmax(z); lr=lr0
       # periodic rebase z to avoid drift
       if iteration % 50 == 49:
           z=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-300)); z-=z.mean()
       # maybe if best exact score? not
    return best, best_score
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    deadline=time.monotonic()+max(0,budget_s-12)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    # lengths
    n_target=6121
    parent_len=None; parent_p=None
    try: ...
    lengths=[]
    if parent_p is not None: lengths.append(parent_p.size)
    for n in [1024, 2048, 4096, n_target]:
       if n not in lengths: lengths.append(n)
    # We might include 512 if time.
    best_p=None; best_score=inf
    # Function update_best(p, score_fft)
    # Phase 1: init scan and short local (30s) for top candidates
    candidates=[]
    for n in lengths:
        inits = _make_initial_pool(n, rng, parent_p if len matches else None)
        for p,s in inits:
            if s < best_score: update
            candidates.append((s,n,p))
    candidates.sort(key=item0)
    # avoid too many same p? We'll take top 8 with diversity length.
    # Run local_search for each top candidate with slice 60s (or 180 if fewer)
    remaining=deadline - now
    tried=set? use hash of rounded p? Hard.
    for s,n,p in candidates[:8]:
        if remaining < 60: break
        slice = min(90, remaining-120)
        p2,s2 = _local_search(p, now+slice, rng,... time_limit maybe 30s?)
        update
    # Main focus on global best with perturbations until deadline-30
    current=best_p.copy(); cur_score=best_score
    attempt=0
    while now < deadline-30:
        slice = min(300, deadline-30-now)
        if attempt>0:
            current = _perturb_log(current, rng, scale=0.3+0.2*attempt)
        p2,s2=_local_search(current, now+slice, rng, ...)
        if s2 < best_score:
            best_p=p2; best_score=s2; current=p2.copy(); attempt=0
        else:
            current=_perturb_log(best_p, rng, scale=0.5+0.2*attempt)
            attempt+=1
        if best_score <= 1.5027? We still can try lower. Continue.
    # final exact up to 5 best? just one
    exact_score=_eval_exact(best_p)
    if not finite: fallback
    return list best

Now, one more mathematical insight: Since p_i nonnegative, score =2n*max diagonal /sum^2. For p normalized to sum1. We can use `score = 2*n*peak`. Maybe use normalized p sum exactly1. In line search, if exact sum differs due floating, score_fft uses p.sum() to be safe.

`_score_fft_fast` implementation:
```
def _score_fft_fast(p):
    p=np.asarray(p)
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return inf
    s=float(np.sum(p))
    if s<=0: return inf
    L=2*n-1; nfft=_next_pow2(L)
    F=np.fft.rfft(p, nfft)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, n=nfft)[:L]
    peak=float(np.max(c))
    if peak<0 and peak> -1e-12: peak=0
    return float(2*n*peak/(s*s))
```
Since p normalized, denom 1. Use `np.nanmax`? if NaN? p finite. But if NaN, return inf. Check finite.

`_conv_self_fft(p)` similar but returns c. It can call `_score`? We can write separate.

To reduce recomputations in `_local_search`, after line search p accepted, next loop recomputes c. Good.

Dense gradient uses `c` from previous. It computes w base**q over all L. If q float, base**q. `base = np.clip(c / peak_c, 0.0, 1.0)` may be slow but okay. For q large, zeros. If `w.sum()` is 0 due base underflow? Peak base=1 so no. If peak_c=0 return zeros.

In `_grad_direct`, using `np.argpartition` each iteration. For n small, K maybe n. Use top K values sorted? We don't need sorted. If K large, slicing `idx=np.arange(L)` maybe. For q weights, all top. Good.
Direct gradient slice addition: p[k::-1] for k=0 gives p[0::-1]? In numpy, `p[0::-1]` gives array [p0] (start at0 step -1 stops at negative), okay. For length1, fine.

Resample function:
```
def _resample_to_length(arr, m):
    n=arr.size
    if n==m: return arr.copy()
    old=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    new=np.linspace(0,1,m)
    y=np.interp(new, old, arr)
    y=y/y.sum()
    return np.clip(y,0,None)
```
Use if parent. This may preserve shape.

```
def _make_random_fourier(n, rng, max_mode=12, amp_scale=0.6, noise=0.0):
    z=np.zeros(n)
    t=np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    for m in range(1,max_mode+1):
        amp=amp_scale * float(rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)) / (m**0.7)
        ph1=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)); ph2=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
        ang=2*np.pi*m*t/n
        z += amp*(np.cos(ang+ph1)+0.5*np.sin(ang+ph2))
    if noise>0: z += rng.normal(0, noise, n)
    return _softmax(z)
```
Using `t=np.arange(n)` each time okay.

Let's specify `_initial_pool` concretely:
```
def _initial_pool(n, rng, parent_p, max_top=8):
    pool=[]
    def add(p): ...
    # ones
    add(np.ones(n))
    # linear lam values
    for lam in (0.3,0.45,0.6,0.71,0.8,0.9):
       x=np.linspace(-1,1,n)
       add((1+lam*x))
       add((1-lam*x))
    # geometric
    for a in (0.5,1,2,4,8):
       t=np.arange(n)/(n-1 if n>1 else1)
       add(np.exp(-a*t)); add(np.exp(a*t))
    # twoexp
    for a in (1,2,4):
       for b in (0.2,1,5):
          add(np.exp(-a*t)+b*np.exp(-a*(1-t)))
    # periodic sawtooth/tri
    for period in (4,8,16,32):
       for amp in (0.5,0.8):
          base = (np.arange(n) % period)/float(period)
          add(1+amp*(2*base-1))
          add(1-amp*(2*base-1))
          add(1+amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*base))
          add(1-amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*base))
    # sinusoids low freq
    for freq in (1,2,3):
       for amp in (0.5,0.7,0.9):
          for phase in (0, np.pi/4, np.pi/2, np.pi):
              add(1+amp*np.sin(2π*freq*np.arange(n)/n + phase))
    # random foriuier
    rand_count = 10 if n>2000 else 20
    for i in range(rand_count):
       add(_make_random_fourier(n,rng, max_mode=8 if n>2000 else 16, amp_scale=0.5+0.3*rng.random()))
    # random exp
    for scale in (0.3,0.7,1.2): add(_make_random_exp(n,rng,scale))
    # binary
    for lam in (0.4,0.7): add(_make_random_binary(n,rng,lam))
```
This might generate many (linear12+geom10+twoexp9+periodic24+sinusoid36+rand~20+bin6=120). For n=6121, each score FFT 16k; 120* maybe 0.02s=2.4s; okay. For multiple lengths maybe 10s. Fine.

```
def _local_search(p0, end_time, rng, q0=4.0, dense_every=10, kfrac=0.12, lr0=0.1):
    p=_normalize(p0)
    if time.monotonic() > end_time - 2.0:
        return p, _score_fft_fast(p)
    n=p.size
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_score_fft_fast(p)
    z=_log_from_p(p)
    lr=lr0
    no_improve=0
    iter_count=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 1.0:
        c=_conv_self_fft(p)
        peak_c=float(np.max(c))
        if peak_c<0: peak_c=0.0
        s=float(np.sum(p)); 
        current=2.0*float(n)*peak_c/(s*s)
        if current < best_score - 1e-12:
             best_score=current; best=p.copy(); no_improve=0
        else: no_improve+=1
        if no_improve > 80:
             z=_log_from_p(best)+rng.normal(0,0.6,n)
             z=z-z.mean(); p=_softmax(z); lr=lr0; no_improve=0; iter_count=0; continue
        elapsed=time.monotonic()-start
        q=max(1.0, q0*(1.0 + elapsed/60.0))
        if q>25: q=25
        if iter_count % dense_every == 0:
             d=_grad_dense(c,p,q)
        else:
             K=max(32, min(n, int(kfrac*n)))
             d=_grad_direct(c,p,q,K)
        dmax=float(np.max(d))
        if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 1e-300:
             break
        ds=d/dmax
        ddot=float(np.dot(p,ds))
        g=p*(ds-ddot)
        rms=float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g*g)))
        if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-300:
             break
        g/=rms
        accepted=False
        step=lr
        for _ in range(6):
            z_try=z-step*g
            z_try=np.clip(z_try, -60.0, 60.0)
            z_try=z_try - float(np.mean(z_try))
            p_try=_softmax(z_try)
            sc=_score_fft_fast(p_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < current - 1e-10*max(1.0,current):
                z=z_try; p=p_try; accepted=True; lr=min(1.5, lr*1.15); break
            step*=0.5
        if not accepted:
            lr*=0.5
            if lr < 1e-5:
                z=_log_from_p(best)+rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
                z=z-z.mean(); p=_softmax(z); lr=lr0
        iter_count+=1
        if iter_count % 50 == 0:
            z=_log_from_p(p)
    return best, _score_fft_fast(best)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import operator

"""
Search for a non-negative step-function coefficient vector minimizing

    2*n*max(convolve(p, p)) / sum(p)^2

using FFT-based proxy scores, smooth-max surrogate gradients in a log/simplex
parameterization, multiple constructive-and-random restarts, and block/mixing
perturbations to escape local minima.  The final sequence is returned after a
short exact-evaluation sanity pass when time allows.
"""


def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _normalize(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / p.size
    return p / s


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / z.size
    return e / s


def _log_from_p(p, eps=1e-300):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    p = np.where(p > eps, p, eps)
    z = np.log(p)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    return z - float(np.mean(z))


def _conv_self_fft(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    nfft = _next_pow2(L)
    f = np.fft.rfft(p, n=nfft)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, n=nfft)[:L].copy()
    return c


def _score_fft_fast(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf")
    c = _conv_self_fft(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def _grad_dense(c, p, q):
    n = p.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    peak_c = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if peak_c <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(peak_c):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    base = np.clip(np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64) / peak_c, 0.0, 1.0)
    w = np.power(base, q)
    ws = float(np.sum(w))
    if ws <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= ws

    r = w[::-1]
    nfft = _next_pow2(n + L - 1)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, n=nfft)
    fr = np.fft.rfft(r, n=nfft)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fp * fr, n=nfft)
    d = 2.0 * conv[n - 1:L][::-1].copy()
    d = np.nan_to_num(d, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(d, 0.0)


def _grad_direct(c, p, q, kmax):
    n = p.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if kmax <= 0:
        kmax = 1
    if kmax >= L:
        kmax = L

    peak_c = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if peak_c <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(peak_c):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if kmax >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, -kmax)[-kmax:]

    vals = c[idx]
    base = np.clip(vals / peak_c, 0.0, 1.0)
    w = np.power(base, q)
    ws = float(np.sum(w))
    if ws <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        w = np.zeros_like(w)
        w[int(np.argmax(vals))] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= ws

    d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for kk, ww in zip(idx, w):
        wk = float(ww)
        if wk <= 1e-300:
            continue
        k = int(kk)
        f = 2.0 * wk
        if k < n:
            d[:k + 1] += f * p[k::-1]
        else:
            start = k - n + 1
            length = L - k
            # p[n-1:start-1:-1] has length exactly `length`
            d[start:start + length] += f * p[n - 1:start - 1:-1]

    d = np.nan_to_num(d, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.maximum(d, 0.0)


def _make_random_exp(n, rng, scale=0.7):
    z = rng.normal(0.0, scale, size=n)
    return _softmax(z)


def _make_random_fourier(n, rng, max_mode=10, amp_scale=0.7, noise_scale=0.0):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, int(max_mode) + 1):
        amp = float(amp_scale) * float(rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0)) / (float(m) ** 0.75)
        ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * m * t / float(n)
        z += amp * (np.cos(ang + ph1) + 0.5 * np.sin(ang + ph2))
    if noise_scale > 0.0:
        z += rng.normal(0.0, noise_scale, size=n)
    return _softmax(z)


def _make_random_binary(n, rng, lam=0.6):
    s = np.where(rng.random(n) < 0.5, 1.0, -1.0)
    s = s - float(np.mean(s))
    p = 1.0 + float(lam) * s
    p = np.clip(p, 1e-12, None)
    return _normalize(p)


def _make_geometric(n, alpha):
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    p = np.exp(-float(alpha) * t)
    return _normalize(p)


def _make_twoexp(n, a, b):
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)
    p = np.exp(-float(a) * t) + float(b) * np.exp(-float(a) * (1.0 - t))
    return _normalize(p)


def _perturb_mixed(base_p, rng, mix, noise_scale):
    base_p = _normalize(base_p)
    n = base_p.size
    r = _make_random_exp(n, rng, noise_scale)
    q = (1.0 - float(mix)) * base_p + float(mix) * r
    q = _normalize(q)
    z = _log_from_p(q) + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=n)
    return _softmax(z)


def _resample_to_length(arr, m):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = arr.size
    if n == 0 or m <= 0:
        return np.ones(m if m > 0 else 1, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == m:
        return arr.copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    y = np.interp(new, old, np.clip(arr, 0.0, None))
    return _normalize(y)


def _initial_pool(n, rng, parent_p):
    pool = []

    def add(p):
        try:
            pp = _normalize(p)
            s = _score_fft_fast(pp)
            if np.isfinite(s):
                pool.append((s, pp))
        except Exception:
            return

    if parent_p is not None and parent_p.size == n:
        add(parent_p)

    add(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    if n > 1:
        x = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, n)
        t = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n - 1)

        # Monotone / skewed profiles.
        for lam in (0.30, 0.45, 0.60, 0.71, 0.80, 0.90):
            add(1.0 + lam * x)
            add(1.0 - lam * x)

        for a in (0.40, 0.60, 0.80, 0.95):
            for b in (1.0, 3.0, 6.0):
                add(1.0 + a * np.tanh(b * x))
                add(1.0 - a * np.tanh(b * x))

        for a in (0.40, 0.70, 0.90):
            for b in (0.20, 0.50, 1.00):
                add(1.0 + a * x + b * x * x * x)

        # Exponential profiles.
        for a in (0.50, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
            add(_make_geometric(n, a))
            add(_make_geometric(n, -a))

        for a in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
            for b in (0.2, 1.0, 5.0):
                add(_make_twoexp(n, a, b))

        # Low-frequency sinusoidal profiles.
        tt = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        for freq in (1, 2, 3):
            for amp in (0.50, 0.70, 0.90):
                for phase in (0.0, 0.4, 1.2, 2.0, 3.1):
                    add(1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * tt + phase))

        # Periodic microstructure.
        for period in (4, 8, 16, 32):
            base = (np.arange(n) % int(period)) / float(period)
            for amp in (0.50, 0.80):
                add(1.0 + amp * (2.0 * base - 1.0))
                add(1.0 - amp * (2.0 * base - 1.0))
                add(1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * base))
                add(1.0 - amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * base))

    # Random smooth starts.
    rand_fourier = 8 if n > 3000 else 16
    for _ in range(rand_fourier):
        add(_make_random_fourier(
            n, rng,
            max_mode=10 if n > 3000 else 16,
            amp_scale=float(rng.uniform(0.4, 0.9)),
            noise_scale=float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.2))
        ))

    for scale in (0.30, 0.70, 1.20):
        add(_make_random_exp(n, rng, scale))

    for lam in (0.40, 0.70):
        add(_make_random_binary(n, rng, lam))

    pool.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    if pool:
        # Flatten near-duplicates by simply keeping top arrays as they are.
        return pool[:10]
    return [(_score_fft_fast(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)), np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))]


def _local_search(p0, end_time, rng, q0=4.0, dense_every=10, kfrac=0.12, lr0=0.10):
    p = _normalize(p0)
    cur_score = _score_fft_fast(p)
    if time.monotonic() > end_time - 2.0:
        return p, cur_score

    n = p.size
    best = p.copy()
    best_score = cur_score

    z = _log_from_p(p)
    lr = float(lr0)
    no_improve = 0
    iter_count = 0
    search_start = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 1.0:
        c = _conv_self_fft(p)
        peak_c = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if peak_c < 0.0:
            peak_c = 0.0
        s = float(np.sum(p))
        current = 2.0 * float(n) * peak_c / (s * s) if s > 0 else float("inf")

        if np.isfinite(current) and current < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = float(current)
            best = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 80:
            p = _perturb_mixed(best, rng, 0.20, 0.70)
            z = _log_from_p(p)
            lr = float(lr0)
            no_improve = 0
            iter_count = 0
            search_start = time.monotonic()
            continue

        elapsed = time.monotonic() - search_start
        q = q0 * (1.0 + elapsed / 60.0)
        if q < 1.0:
            q = 1.0
        if q > 25.0:
            q = 25.0

        if iter_count % int(dense_every) == 0:
            d = _grad_dense(c, p, q)
        else:
            kint = max(32, min(n, int(float(kfrac) * float(n))))
            d = _grad_direct(c, p, q, kint)

        dmax = float(np.max(d)) if d.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 1e-300:
            break

        ds = d / dmax
        ddot = float(np.dot(p, ds))
        g = p * (ds - ddot)
        rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(g * g)))
        if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-300:
            break
        g /= rms

        accepted = False
        step = lr
        for _ in range(6):
            z_try = z - step * g
            z_try = np.clip(z_try, -60.0, 60.0)
            z_try = z_try - float(np.mean(z_try))
            p_try = _softmax(z_try)
            sc = _score_fft_fast(p_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < current - 1e-10 * max(1.0, current):
                z = z_try
                p = p_try
                accepted = True
                lr = min(1.50, lr * 1.15)
                break
            step *= 0.5

        if not accepted:
            lr *= 0.5
            if lr < 1e-5:
                p = _perturb_mixed(best, rng, 0.30, 0.90)
                z = _log_from_p(p)
                lr = float(lr0)
                search_start = time.monotonic()

        iter_count += 1
        if iter_count % 50 == 0:
            z = _log_from_p(p)

    return best, _score_fft_fast(best)


def _add_candidate(store, p, score, limit=10):
    try:
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            return
        store.append((float(score), np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()))
        if len(store) > int(limit):
            worst = 0
            worst_s = store[0][0]
            for i in range(1, len(store)):
                if store[i][0] > worst_s:
                    worst_s = store[i][0]
                    worst = i
            store.pop(worst)
    except Exception:
        pass


def _top_candidates(store, count):
    res = []
    work = list(store)
    for _ in range(int(count)):
        if not work:
            break
        idx = 0
        best = work[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(work)):
            if work[i][0] < best:
                best = work[i][0]
                idx = i
        res.append(work[idx])
        work.pop(idx)
    return res


def _eval_exact_guard(p):
    try:
        vals = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)]
        s = float(evaluate_sequence(vals))
        if np.isfinite(s):
            return s
    except Exception:
        pass
    return float("inf")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Time-bounded multi-start search for a low-peaked non-negative self-convolution.
    Returns the best valid finite-length list found.
    """
    fallback_n = 6121
    fallback = np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        total = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 2400.0
        if total <= 5.0:
            return [float(x) for x in fallback]

        start = time.monotonic()
        deadline = start + max(1.0, total - 18.0)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

        parent_p = None
        try:
            raw_parent = height_sequence_1
            parent_arr = np.asarray(raw_parent, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
            if parent_arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(parent_arr)):
                parent_p = _normalize(parent_arr)
        except Exception:
            parent_p = None

        if parent_p is not None and parent_p.size > 20000:
            parent_p = _resample_to_length(parent_p, fallback_n)

        base_lengths = [fallback_n, 4096, 2048, 1024]
        lengths = []
        for n in base_lengths:
            if n not in lengths:
                lengths.append(n)
        if parent_p is not None and 512 <= parent_p.size <= 10000 and parent_p.size not in lengths:
            lengths.append(parent_p.size)

        # Deterministic order: the reference/target length first, then shorter lengths.
        lengths.sort(key=lambda n: (n != fallback_n, n))
        # Remove lambda above by replacing with a small key helper? We can't use lambda, so rebuild:
        lengths_sorted = []
        for n in sorted(lengths, reverse=True):
            if n == fallback_n:
                lengths_sorted.append(n)
        for n in sorted(lengths, reverse=True):
            if n != fallback_n and n not in lengths_sorted:
                lengths_sorted.append(n)
        lengths = lengths_sorted

        store = []
        best_p = None
        best_fft_score = float("inf")

        # Phase 0: initial construction scan.
        length_results = []
        for n in lengths:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
                break
            par = parent_p if (parent_p is not None and parent_p.size == n) else None
            top = _initial_pool(n, rng, par)
            length_results.append((n, top))
            for s, p in top[:4]:
                if s < best_fft_score:
                    best_fft_score = s
                    best_p = p.copy()
                _add_candidate(store, p, s, 12)

        if best_p is None:
            best_p = np.ones(fallback_n, dtype=np.float64)
            best_fft_score = _score_fft_fast(best_p)
            _add_candidate(store, best_p, best_fft_score, 12)

        print(f"initial best: n={best_p.size}, fft_score={best_fft_score:.10f}", flush=True)

        # Phase 1: short local searches on promising initial states.
        if length_results:
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic()
            per = max(30.0, min(120.0, remaining / float(max(1, len(length_results)))))
            for n, top in length_results:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
                    break
                if not top:
                    continue
                end = min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + per)
                p0, s0 = top[0]
                try:
                    p2, s2 = _local_search(p0, end, rng)
                    if s2 < best_fft_score:
                        best_fft_score = s2
                        best_p = p2.copy()
                    _add_candidate(store, p2, s2, 12)
                    print(f"short local n={n}: score={s2:.10f}", flush=True)
                except Exception:
                    pass

        # Phase 2: concentrate remaining time on best / perturbed best states.
        current = best_p.copy()
        cur_score = best_fft_score
        attempt = 0

        while time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
            if best_p is None:
                break
            remaining = deadline - 45.0 - time.monotonic()
            if remaining < 30.0:
                break
            slice_s = min(300.0, remaining)
            if attempt == 0:
                current = best_p.copy()
            else:
                mix = min(0.45, 0.15 + 0.05 * attempt)
                current = _perturb_mixed(best_p, rng, mix, float(0.6 + 0.15 * attempt))

            end = min(deadline - 45.0, time.monotonic() + slice_s)
            try:
                p2, s2 = _local_search(current, end, rng)
            except Exception:
                break

            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < best_fft_score:
                best_fft_score = s2
                best_p = p2.copy()
                attempt = 0
                _add_candidate(store, best_p, best_fft_score, 12)
                print(f"main update: n={best_p.size}, fft_score={best_fft_score:.10f}", flush=True)
            else:
                attempt += 1

            if attempt >= 8:
                break

        _add_candidate(store, best_p, best_fft_score, 12)

        # Phase 3: exact final selection among only the very top FFT candidates.
        final_best_p = best_p
        final_best_score = best_fft_score
        exact_best_p = None
        exact_best_score = float("inf")

        exact_deadline = time.monotonic() + 18.0
        top = _top_candidates(store, 4)
        # Make sure the current best is checked first.
        checked = []
        if best_p is not None:
            checked.append(best_p)
        for _, p in top:
            if p is not None and all(p != x for x in checked):
                checked.append(p)

        for p in checked:
            if time.monotonic() > exact_deadline:
                break
            try:
                es = _eval_exact_guard(p)
                if np.isfinite(es) and es < exact_best_score:
                    exact_best_score = es
                    exact_best_p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

        if exact_best_p is not None:
            final_best_p = exact_best_p
            final_best_score = exact_best_score
            print(f"final exact best: n={final_best_p.size}, score={final_best_score:.10f}", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"final fft best: n={final_best_p.size}, score={final_best_fft_score:.10f}", flush=True)

        out = np.asarray(final_best_p, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(np.sum(out))
        if s <= 0.0:
            out = fallback.copy()
        else:
            out = out / s

        # Final exact sanity check. If it fails or is absurdly bad, use fallback.
        final_score = _eval_exact_guard(out)
        if not np.isfinite(final_score) or final_score >= 2.0:
            # Try fallback constant; it is guaranteed to score 2 (unless evaluator differs).
            fb_score = _eval_exact_guard(fallback)
            if np.isfinite(fb_score) and (not np.isfinite(final_score) or fb_score < final_score):
                out = fallback.copy()
                final_score = fb_score
            print(f"final guard score: {final_score}", flush=True)
        else:
            print(f"final returned score: {final_score:.10f}, n={out.size}", flush=True)

        return [float(x) for x in out]

    except Exception:
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]
```
